Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the formal_languages space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 578 and the convergence rate is O(949/n^1).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 578 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the formal_languages structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 949. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 214.7 (Homomorphism Theorem)

Theorem 214.995 (Perturbation Bound)

Statement: For any formal_languages module M satisfying the perturbation bound hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 772. The projective resolution has length at most 816 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 772 is immediate from the perturbation bound property. For the inductive step, we decompose the formal_languages structure using the perturbation bound theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 816. Each subproblem has a solution by the perturbation bound fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
